# Roth/CoeffNorm.lean / Roth/Index.lean

494 comparisons among the 293 accepted declarations. [Source hashes and review provenance](CoeffNorm--Index.json).

| Declaration A | Declaration B | Same statement? | Same proof / mathematical relationship? | Recommended action |

| `Roth.index` | `Roth.normOne` | no | A: Defines the index as the minimum reciprocal-weighted exponent among nonzero Taylor coefficients, using real infimum with zero value at the zero polynomial. Mechanism: Takes sInf of the image of the finite support of the project taylor under weight. B: Defines the sum of absolute values of the integer coefficients, valued in the real numbers. Mechanism: Sums coefficient magnitudes over the finite monomial support. Coefficient norms/evaluation estimates control magnitudes of nonzero coefficients or values. Index controls the smallest reciprocal weight at which a Taylor coefficient is nonzero, independently of the magnitude of that coefficient. Multiplication laws use different mechanisms: triangle/submultiplicativity for l1 norm versus noncancellation of the leading weighted order for exact index additivity. Scalar-zero and zero-polynomial conventions cannot be transferred between these scalar invariants. | Keep height/magnitude bounds distinct from vanishing-order bounds; coordinate finite-support and coefficient transport primitives only where they actually coincide. |
